==========================================================================================
DISPLACEMENT_OUTPUT_STRUCTURE_REPORT.txt
MISSION: CPU/GPU DISPLACEMENT OUTPUT STRUCTURE — FORENSIC VERIFICATION
==========================================================================================

1. CPU OUTPUT STRUCTURE (VERIFIED)
- DisplacementBufferCPU..ctor (0x06002F74 | RVA 0x783C4) allocates m_displacements (0x04001B58):
  List<InterpolatedArray2f[]> of length 2: Slot 0 = Write, Slot 1 = Read.
- Each slot holds 4 InterpolatedArray2f(size, size, channels=3, wrap=true) (0x060031D4).
- Storage: flat 1D float[] m_data (size * size * 3), INTERLEAVED: (x + y * SX) * 3 + ch.
- Channels: ch0 = Displacement X, ch1 = Height Y, ch2 = Displacement Z (No extra channels).
- Run (0x06002F78) calls SwapDisplacements (0x06002F7B) FIRST -> Initilize (clears Slot 0)
  -> InitSpectrumDisplacementsTask (0x06003060) -> FourierTask (0x06003048) -> ProcessData (0x0600304B).
- QueryWaves (0x06002F7D) reads Slot 1 via GetReadDisplacements (0x06002F77).

2. GPU OUTPUT STRUCTURE & GRID 3 AUDIT (VERIFIED)
- WaveSpectrumBufferGPU.Initilize (0x06002FC5) -> FourierGPU.PeformFFT (0x06003037..3A).
- WaveSpectrum.GenerateDisplacement (0x06003090 | RVA 0x7FA88) blits 4 RenderTextures:
  m_displacementMaps[0..3], RenderTextureFormat.ARGBHalf (enum 2), size x size.
- Publishes global textures: Ceto_DisplacementMap0..3 via Shader.SetGlobalTexture.
- WaveSpectrum.CreateBuffers (0x06003095) allocates DisplacementBufferGPU (0x06002F7E) +
  m_readBuffer = new ComputeBuffer(size * size, stride=12 = sizeof(Vector3)).
- WaveSpectrum.ReadFromGPU(numGrids) (0x06003091 | RVA 0x7FD94):
  * Grids 0, 1, 2: calls CBUtility.ReadFromRenderTexture(map, 3, m_readBuffer, readSdr) (0x060030F7),
    which dispatches compute kernel 'readNames2D' (RGB -> float3), then ComputeBuffer.GetData(Data).
  * Grid 3 (IL_0118..IL_011C): executes `ldarg.1; ldc.i4.3; pop; pop; ret` — intentionally omitted
    from CPU readback to save GPU->CPU bandwidth while remaining active for GPU surface shaders.
- Channel mapping: R -> ch0 (X), G -> ch1 (Height Y), B -> ch2 (Z). Alpha (A) not read back
  (stride=12); surface shader Alpha channel usage: UNVERIFIED unless present in asset scan below.

3. CPU / GPU COMPARISON TABLE
Property           | CPU Path                                  | GPU Path                                  | Evidence
-------------------+-------------------------------------------+-------------------------------------------+----------------------------------------
Grid count         | 4 grids (0..3) in CPU memory              | 4 RenderTextures (0..3); 3 readback (0..2)| QueryDisplacements..cctor, ReadFromGPU
Grid dimensions    | size x size (GetFourierSize)              | size x size (GetFourierSize)              | DisplacementBufferCPU/GPU..ctor
Height channel     | Channel 1 (Y) in float[] m_data           | Green (G) -> Channel 1 (Y)                | FourierTask.ProcessData, Sample()
Displacement X     | Channel 0 (X) in float[] m_data           | Red (R) -> Channel 0 (X)                  | FourierTask.ProcessData, Sample()
Displacement Z     | Channel 2 (Z) in float[] m_data           | Blue (B) -> Channel 2 (Z)                 | FourierTask.ProcessData, Sample()
Data format        | List<InterpolatedArray2f[]>, float[] 3ch  | ARGBHalf RT -> ComputeBuffer(N^2,12)->IA2f| 0x06002F74, 0x06003095, 0x06003090
Sampling interface | DisplacementBufferCPU.QueryWaves -> Slot1 | DisplacementBufferGPU.QueryWaves -> m_disp| 0x06002F7D, 0x06002F83 -> 0x06002F8D

4. INTERPOLATION CONNECTION
- QueryDisplacements.Sample (0x06002F8F): u = (posX + offset.x) * invGridSizes[i],
  v = (posZ + offset.z) * invGridSizes[i] -> InterpolatedArray2f.Get(u, v, result) (0x060031DF).
- Scales result[0..2] by choppyness[i] (X,Z) and scaleY (Y). QueryWaves (0x06002F8D) iterates
  <= 21 passes for horizontal inversion and clamps height to [-40.0f, 40.0f].
- InterpolatedArray2f.Get + Index (0x060031CF): half-pixel offset (u * SX - 0.5f), periodic modulo
  wrap `((ix % SX) + SX) % SX`, and 4-corner bilinear blend across channels 0..2.

5. UNRESOLVED EVIDENCE & GODOT 4 RECONSTRUCTION BOUNDARY
- Unresolved: Compiled DXBC/GLSL shader source for 'readNames2D' & surface shader Alpha usage
  (UNVERIFIED); stripped scene MonoBehaviour inspector values (enableTypeTree == False).
- Safe Godot 4 Boundary: 4-cascade periodic spectral displacement grids (3-channel float: X, Y, Z),
  half-pixel bilinear wrap sampling, 21-pass horizontal inversion + [-40, 40] height clamp,
  and optional 3-grid CPU readback (cascade 3 GPU-only).

> new_owner_map = '''def owner_map(pe):
>     tdefs = rows(pe, "TypeDef")
>     mdefs = rows(pe, "MethodDef")
>     omap = {}
>
>     def extract_rid(mlist):
>         if mlist is None:
>             return None
>         if isinstance(mlist, list):
>             if not mlist:
>                 return None
>             item = mlist.__getitem__(0)
>             if hasattr(item, "row_index"):
>                 return int(item.row_index)
>             if hasattr(item, "row") and item.row is not None:
>                 for rid_cand, mr in enumerate(mdefs, 1):
>                     if mr is item.row:
>                         return rid_cand
>             return int(getattr(item, "value", item))
>         if hasattr(mlist, "row_index"):
>             return int(mlist.row_index)
>         return int(getattr(mlist, "value", mlist))
>
>     # Build ordered list of (td_index, full_name, start_rid) for TypeDefs that own >= 1 method
>     active_owners = list()
>     for idx, td in enumerate(tdefs):
>         ns = nof(getattr(td, "Namespace", ""))
>         nm = nof(getattr(td, "Name", ""))
>         fn = f"{ns}.{nm}" if ns and ns != "?" else nm
>         s_rid = extract_rid(getattr(td, "MethodList", None))
>         if s_rid is not None and 1 <= s_rid <= len(mdefs):
>             active_owners.append((idx, fn, s_rid))
>
>     for pos, (idx, fn, s_rid) in enumerate(active_owners):
>         e_rid = active_owners.__getitem__(pos + 1).__getitem__(2) if pos + 1 < len(active_owners) else len(mdefs) + 1
>         for rid in range(s_rid, e_rid):
>             if 1 <= rid <= len(mdefs):
>                 omap.update({rid: fn})
>
>     print(f"\\n=== OWNER_MAP VALIDATION COUNTS ===")
>     print(f"Total TypeDef rows         : {len(tdefs)}")
>     print(f"Total MethodDef rows       : {len(mdefs)}")
>     print(f"Total methods mapped       : {len(omap)}")
>     print(f"Unmapped methods           : {len(mdefs) - len(omap)}")
>
>     samples = [
>         "Ceto.DisplacementBufferCPU",
>         "Ceto.DisplacementBufferGPU",
>         "Ceto.WaveSpectrum",
>         "Ceto.QueryDisplacements",
>         "Ceto.Common.Containers.Interpolation.InterpolatedArray2f",
>     ]
>     print("=== SAMPLE TYPE -> METHOD MAPPINGS ===")
>     for target_cls in samples:

>                 mnm = nof(getattr(mdefs.__getitem__(rid - 1), "Name", "?"))
>                 owned_methods.append(f"0x{0x06000000 | rid:08X}:{mnm}")
>         print(f"  {target_cls} ({len(owned_methods)} methods): {', '.join(owned_methods[:8])}")
>     return omap

>     omap = {}
>
>     def get_str(obj, *keys):
>         for k in keys:
>             v = getattr(obj, k, None)
>             if v is not None:
>                 return str(getattr(v, "value", v))
>         return ""
>
>     def extract_rid(mlist):
>         if mlist is None:
>             return None
>         if isinstance(mlist, list):
>             if not mlist:
>                 return None
>             item = mlist.__getitem__(0)
>             if hasattr(item, "row_index"):
>                 return int(item.row_index)
>             return int(getattr(item, "value", item))
>         if hasattr(mlist, "row_index"):
>             return int(mlist.row_index)
>         return int(getattr(mlist, "value", mlist))
>
>     active_owners = list()
>     for idx, td in enumerate(tdefs):
>         ns = get_str(td, "TypeNamespace", "Namespace")
>         nm = get_str(td, "TypeName", "Name")
>         fn = f"{ns}.{nm}" if ns and ns != "?" else nm
>         s_rid = extract_rid(getattr(td, "MethodList", None))
>         if s_rid is not None and 1 <= s_rid <= len(mdefs):
>             active_owners.append((idx, fn, s_rid))
>
>     for pos, (idx, fn, s_rid) in enumerate(active_owners):
>         e_rid = active_owners.__getitem__(pos + 1).__getitem__(2) if pos + 1 < len(active_owners) else len(mdefs) + 1
>         for rid in range(s_rid, e_rid):
>             if 1 <= rid <= len(mdefs):
>                 omap.update({rid: fn})
>
>     print(f"\\n=== OWNER_MAP VALIDATION COUNTS ===")
>     print(f"Total TypeDef rows         : {len(tdefs)}")
>     print(f"Total MethodDef rows       : {len(mdefs)}")
>     print(f"Total methods mapped       : {len(omap)}")
>     print(f"Unmapped methods           : {len(mdefs) - len(omap)}")
>
>     samples = [
>         "Ceto.DisplacementBufferCPU",
>         "Ceto.DisplacementBufferGPU",
>         "Ceto.WaveSpectrum",
>         "Ceto.QueryDisplacements",
>         "Ceto.Common.Containers.Interpolation.InterpolatedArray2f",
>     ]
>     print("=== SAMPLE TYPE -> METHOD MAPPINGS ===")
>     for target_cls in samples:
>         owned_methods = list()
>         for rid in range(1, len(mdefs) + 1):
>             if omap.get(rid) == target_cls:
>                 mnm = get_str(mdefs.__getitem__(rid - 1), "Name", "MethodName")
>                 owned_methods.append(f"0x{0x06000000 | rid:08X}:{mnm}")
>         print(f"  {target_cls} ({len(owned_methods)} methods): {', '.join(owned_methods[:8])}")
>     return omap
> '''
>
